# ATLAS refresh: per-entry field-level delta

ATLAS pin 2026.06 -> 2026.09. Before = `origin/main:data/incidents.json (12f0a98f)`, after = `data/incidents.json on branch ws4/v2130-atlas-refresh after make build`. Generated by `scripts/atlas_entry_delta.py`; do not hand-edit. (DO-NOT-REGENERATE once committed: it is a dated record of one refresh.)

## Declared context (written by the pipeline engineer, 2026-10-06)

> **Correction note, 2026-10-09 (figure corrected in place; red-reviewer BOUNCE #1 on
> `df61dcad`, defect 2).** This section first said **2,540** entries had a superseded
> technique id translated. That number was a sum of per-id occurrences
> (2,498 `AML.T0058` + 41 `AML.T0019` + 1 `AML.T0015.001`), not a count of entries:
> 8 entries carry BOTH `AML.T0019` and `AML.T0058` (the "3 + 3 + 2" rows in the pattern
> table below), so they were counted twice. Per entity the figure is **2,532** entries
> whose technique set changed; 2,532 + 3,309 tactics-only = **5,841**, which is the
> total the rest of this record already used and which is unchanged. The per-id counts
> (2,498 / 41 / 1) remain true as occurrences. No data changed; only this prose figure.
> Re-derivation (prints `2532 3309`; needs the `12f0a98f` base commit and the branch's
> `data/incidents.json`):
> `python -c "import json,subprocess as s;L=lambda t:{e['id']:e for e in json.loads(t)['incidents']};o=L(s.check_output(['git','show','12f0a98f:data/incidents.json']));n=L(open('data/incidents.json',encoding='utf-8').read());S=lambda e,k:set(e.get(k) or []);print(sum(S(o[i],'mitre_atlas')!=S(n[i],'mitre_atlas') for i in o),sum(S(o[i],'mitre_atlas')==S(n[i],'mitre_atlas') and S(o[i],'mitre_atlas_tactics')!=S(n[i],'mitre_atlas_tactics') for i in o))"`

**What changed and why.** The pinned ATLAS release moved 2026.06 -> 2026.09
(`ingest/atlas/ATLAS-2026.09.yaml`, sha256 in `ingest/atlas/ATLAS.provenance.json`).
Two mechanisms change entry codes, and only these two:

1. **Superseded technique ids (mechanical translation, 2,532 entries; corrected from 2,540, see the dated correction note above).**
   `AML.T0019` -> `AML.T0115.000` (41 entries), `AML.T0058` -> `AML.T0115.001`
   (2,498), `AML.T0104` -> `AML.T0115.002` (0 entries carried it),
   `AML.T0015.001` -> `AML.T0015` (1 entry, `INC-06842`; that id is undefined in 2026.06
   AND 2026.09, a pre-existing AVID-ingest defect the new lint surfaced). The table is
   `mappings/atlas_id_translations.json`, applied in `merge_and_dedupe.py::fill_taxonomy`
   (fresh rows) and `refresh_atlas_codes` (retained priors, which bypass fill_taxonomy).
   The mapping HEURISTICS that emit these ids (`LLM_TO_ATLAS`, `ASI_TO_ATLAS`, ...) are
   unchanged; note they still emit the old ids and rely on the translation (e.g. LLM07
   -> `AML.T0058` -> `AML.T0115.001`, and `AML.T0115.001` is 'Models' under 'Publish
   Poisoned AI Artifacts', a semantically odd target for LLM07 that the heuristic always
   had; flagged for label-scientist, not changed here).
2. **Technique -> tactic link changes in the new release** (re-derivation through the
   unchanged union logic). From the release diff: `AML.T0012` and `AML.T0053` gained
   `AML.TA0015`; `AML.T0020` lost `AML.TA0003`; `AML.T0065` and `AML.T0066` moved
   `AML.TA0003` -> `AML.TA0001`. Entries rebuilt from sources pick this up fully
   (3,309 entries change tactics only). Retained priors get tactics ADDED only (union),
   never removed: a retained prior that already carried `AML.TA0003` for one of those
   techniques keeps it. Tactic `AML.TA0001` was also renamed 'AI Attack Staging' ->
   'AI Attack Adaptation' (name only; ids unchanged; this is in the pin, not in entries).

**Other fields that move, by design.** `updated` is stamped with the build date (2026-10-06)
by the merge whenever an entry's content snapshot changes; `last_seen` is derived from
`updated`. These two moved on exactly the changed-code entries and on no others (checked
per entity below). Top-level `generated` moves for the same reason. Regenerated
artifacts (all pure functions of `data/incidents.json`): `data/incidents.min.json`,
`docs/data/incidents.min.json`, `src/genai_incidents/data/incidents.min.json`,
`INCIDENTS.md`, `docs/incidents/*.md` shards (22), `README.md` (the `built` date only).
`data/stats.json` gains `taxonomy_versions` and its `generated` moves. No entry was
added, removed, tombstoned or re-IDed.

**Independent check (agreement 6).** Not the tool's own method: a separate script that
reads origin/main's `incidents.json`, this branch's, and the RAW upstream YAML (not the
pin) verified, per entry, that the after technique set equals the translation of the
before set, that every tactic derivable from the after techniques via the release's own
`achieves` relationships is present, that no tactic appears that neither the new links
nor the before set explain, and that entries it counts as unchanged are canonical-JSON
identical. It first found 2 entries (`INC-08463`, `INC-08923`, retained priors) missing
the new `AML.TA0015` link; that is what produced `refresh_atlas_codes`.

## Entry count and ID set

- before: **15666** entries; after: **15666** entries.
- ids added: -; ids removed: -.
- ID set identical: **True**.
